# fr4735-monluc-lansac-poland-1573 (check-solved, NC-MONL, 7 Oct 2026)

Slug as the brief. Status: **partial**. The Lansac half is READ by another solver (found-solved for that half: F-class "another solver"); the Monluc half (Cipher 1: ff. 84, 87, 132, 136, 138; Cipher 2: f. 209) is the only residue and is unmeasured.

## Sources checked

BnF fr. 4735 no. 51 f. 124 and the siblings ff. 154, 156, 160, 164, 174", status `complete`.** Quote: "Every cipher letter of Lanssac's in fr. 4735 is over the read bar, measured per letter ... f. 124 96 %, ... ff. 154r-v 97.5 %, ff. 156r-v 95.8 %, f. 160 100 %, f. 164 98.1 %, f. 174 100 %; the 65 open tokens are gutter-cut ... ## Verdict reasoning
Lansac's six cipher letters: read by Bourdeau (H/C-style crib from the leaf's own gloss plus controls; measured 95.8-100 % per letter) -- do not promote. Monluc: a different system (Tomokiyo: nulls that look like words, jargon substitutions), not read by either solver repository, and no print decipherment found by whole-volume Noailles grep, but (i) the number and length of Monluc cipher passages is unmeasured, (ii) each may carry the same marginal decipherment as Lansac's, which would make it known text, (iii) six sources were not all run. Expected value falls well below the scout's 1.28-type estimate: the pool the scout counted (about 8000 signs) is mostly Lansac's already-read letters.

## Verdict (NC-MONL2)
Status stays `partial`. The Lanssac half is Bourdeau's (above). The Monluc half has at least one cipher letter with a contemporary decipherment on the leaf (f.86, about 165 signs: known-answer material) and about 3,950 estimated signs on four full cipher pages with no gloss on the page; whether f.139 and ff.211-223 are in-volume decipherments of them is the one thing that sets the residue between about 500 and about 3,950 signs. The residue is therefore at or above the brief's 1,500-sign / 2-letter line only on the upper bound; the intake gate exits 0 and `specs/fr4735-monluc-lansac-poland-1573.json` is written with that caveat in its constraints. No test run.

## NA-MONL test 1 (7 Oct 2026, Opus, for LANE NEWT-A-account-1)
Spec test 1, known-answer. No c258-c270 read (test 2). Lansac untouched.
- **Key.** `key.tsv`: Tomokiyo's Monluc Cipher 1 table (sources/cryptiana/web/henryiii_Monluc1.png, his caveat "my transcription may not be accurate"), cut into 68 cells K01-K68 (`images/keysheet_monluc1_ids.png`, ids only, no letters, so the transcription passes were blind to plaintext). Key source: **published** (Tomokiyo). Cells K54 (faint grey), K62 (f.85 note) and K64-K68 (annotation row "f.132": C3B, a, @, h-bar) are M: their column placement was read off the image and their meaning is uncertain.
- **Images.** Gallica answered (HTTP 200, 01:25 UTC). `python3 tools/iiif_lines.py --ark btv1b9060724s --canvas 172 --region 1230,950,2580,520 --out images --prefix c172cipher --debug` (4 lines: L01 is clear text, L02-L04 cipher) and `... --region 560,960,800,900 --prefix c172gloss --debug` (margin). Note: with a 2580 px region the s2 segment overlaps s1 by about 2220 px, not 150; pass A read s1 only, and nothing is lost since s1 runs to the paper edge on L02-L03 and past the end of L04.
- **Transcription.** Two blind Sonnet passes on crops (passes/passA.tsv, passB.tsv): 83.6% agree (127/152). `tools/reconcile_passes.py` gave 25 disagreements, settled by fixed pair rules (passes/settled.tsv), each decided on one image instance before scoring: K11 over K62 (double crossbar), K18 over K15 (large C), K14 over K49 (6-shape), otherwise pass A. Result `ciphertext.txt`: 150 sign tokens plus 2 END_CUT markers (lines 60/61/31 incl. markers), of which 2 are "?vertical_bar".
- **Gloss.** `gloss_c172.txt`: "tesmoignage / ny eust / quelque auanc / e faudroit / quil neust point / charge de negoc / rien qui soit / smon de / rendre tesmoign" (96 letters normalised).
- **Score** (`python3 score_c172.py`, `--check` for rule 7; semi-global alignment, one normalisation j->i v->u y->i): target **0.435** (64/147) vs shuffled-token control (200 draws) mean 0.255, p95 0.279, max 0.313. Per pass: A 0.422, B 0.410, both above the control max. N about 150, so this is a thin but clean known-answer pass, not a full reading.
- **Observation (exploratory, post hoc):** the decode of L02 contains "...alarochete que puur empepche", i.e. the clear line 1 of the leaf ("& mondt seigneur nest allé a la Rochelle que pour empesch"), and starts "gepmuigae" ~ "tesmoignage". So the margin "tesmoignage" plus line 1 look like the decipherment of L02, and the remaining margin lines that of L03-L04 ("aieupg quelque ... faudroi g quil aeupg poiacg charg de ..." / "... rendre ... gepmogaag" = tesmoignage). With line 1 inserted (gloss_c172_withline1.txt): 0.640 vs control max 0.347. Not pre-registered; reported for the next worker only.
- **Where the table and the leaf disagree (from the decode, not settled):** "tesmoignage" decodes "gepmuigaer" -> the first sign (K07, '2' = g in the table) stands for t here, K14 (b-shape = p) for s, K39 (y = u) for o in several places. Either the passes chose look-alike cells or Tomokiyo's table misplaces these; a cell-by-cell check against the gloss is the next step (it would make the key period-checked, grade C, on this leaf).
Grade counts on the reading: none claimed (no reading is asserted; this is a score of a published key). Requests: gallica.bnf.fr 4 (manifest probe, info.json, 1 overview, 2 native regions), 1.5 s+ apart.
Suggested next: (1) per-cell C-grade correction of key.tsv from the f.86 gloss+line 1 alignment (~$2); (2) then spec test 2 (c268 vs c270).

## MONLUC-KEY step 1: per-cell key check on f.86 (7 Oct 2026, Opus, for the account-3 orchestrator)
Brief `.claude/briefs/runs/2026-10-07-acct3-monluc-xmatch.md` part A. Script `check_cells.py` (`--check` for rule 7) aligns
token by token (score_c172.py's scoring, with traceback) against `gloss_c172_withline1.txt`: the margin gloss plus the clear
line 1. NA-MONL marked that insertion as exploratory, and the same is said here. Each cell is then judged on the gloss letters its tokens face.
- **Cells (68):** confirm 15 (K03 c, K04 d, K05 e, K08 h, K09 i, K10 l, K11 m, K13 o, K15 q, K16 r, K26 e, K39 u, K41 a, K42 e,
  K49 r); contradict 2 (K01 a->n 8/12; K18 t->l 2/2); mixed 2 (K07 g: faces t 7, g 5, n 1, s 1; K14 p: faces s 7, p 3, e 1, t 1);
  once 2 (K06 f agrees; K62 l faces m); unexercised 47. **Shuffle control** (200 token shuffles, same procedure):
  confirm mean 1.5, p95 3; contradict mean 0.42, p95 1. The 15 confirmations stand well clear of chance.
- **Gain gate (held-out line):** corrections learned on two lines, scored on the third, against the same corrections on shuffled
  held-out tokens: L02 +6 (shuffle p95 4), L03 0 (p95 0), L04 +3 (p95 3). The gain clears its control on 1 line of 3, so the
  gate is **not passed**. key.tsv is unchanged, still Tomokiyo's table as published.
- **Attribution (ciphertext_c172.tsv, per position with passes A/B):** K18->l are the two L02 tokens where the passes split
  K15/K18 (pass A q, pass B t), and the gloss says l. Neither pass found that cell, so the sign is unidentified, not a table error.
  K01->n: 3 of 8 n-facing tokens are where pass B read K12 (n) and the pair rule chose K01; the other 5 are tokens both
  passes called K01. K07 and K14 split only on tokens both passes agree on. Image look at c172cipher_L02_s1.jpg (2000 px): the t-facing
  K07 at L02:1 and the g-facing K07 at L02:7 look like the same 'z/2' form to this reader. Either the leaf uses one sign for
  two letters (a homophone or a merge not in the table), or the difference sits below crop resolution. **Not settled:** it needs native
  crops of the individual signs (K01/K12, K07, K14 instances), one look each, about $1.
- **Graded reading (rule 4):** `decode.json` -> `python3 tools/decode_key.py ciphers/fr4735-monluc-lansac-poland-1573 [--check]`
  writes reading_c172.txt / reading_tokens_c172.tsv: **C 76, M 71, U 3** of 150 (C = table letter equals the gloss letter it
  faces; M = differs, faces no gloss letter, or the passes split; U = '?vertical_bar'). No H claimed. tools/decode_key.py
  learned 'plain' as a key value-column name (one alias; its offline test shows the same 3 pre-existing failures,
  antt-linhares-chave and rah-canada-1869, with and without the change).
- **Depth candidate:** D1 at most on this leaf (scattered words, e.g. "a la roche[lle] que pour empesche" on cells already
  confirmed). The leaf's own gloss is the plaintext, so nothing here is a reading of unglossed text.

## MONLUC-KEY test 2: c268 (f.138) lines 1-5 with the published table (7 Oct 2026, Opus, for the account-3 orchestrator)
Scope: one leaf, the first 5 cipher lines only (~175 signs of the page's ~1,050), priced as 3 units (2 blind passes and the
reconciliation) plus one clear-text reading of c270. Crops: the native region `src_ark_..._f268_700_1500_2800_650.jpg` (one
Gallica fetch). `tools/iiif_lines.py --follow-slope` split this slanted page wrongly (it doubled two lines and missed one; debug overlay
checked), so the five bands were cut by a manual shear (a = -0.032), and the method is recorded in images/manifest.json. c270: region
500,1050,3050,1300 cut into 4 bands. Gallica requests this pass: 2 overviews + 2 info/region, at least 1.5 s apart, plus one reset retried once.
- **Transcription.** passes/c268_passA.tsv and c268_passB.tsv are blind Sonnet passes against keysheet_monluc1_ids.png, and agree on
  130/177 positions (73%; pass B's L03 stops after 18 signs). Pass A is the base, and every position pass B does not match is conf M
  (ciphertext_c268.tsv). 27% disagreement is above TRANSCRIPTION.md's one-tenth line, so a further machine pass is not the next
  step (see gaps).
- **Decode** (`score_c268.py`, `--check` for rule 7; reading_c268.txt, table unchanged, W: words dropped as nulls). L01 reads
  "lesperuiteursde [mihil] [Cur] quinpespargnenulhon", and pass B alone gives the same line. Not graded, and no reading is claimed:
  the gate below cannot license one.
- **Judge (fr16 corpus, spec key 'fr').** The decode FAILs: language -1.426 vs real_p05 -0.901 (null_p99 -1.799), word cover 0.84 vs min 0.5
  (pass). **Shuffled-decode control** (same tokens shuffled, same key, 100 draws): 0/100 PASS, best language score -1.749, so the
  decode is above every shuffle. **Positive control (calibration):** the f.86 decode with the same table, a decode the leaf's own gloss
  confirms (0.640 vs shuffle max 0.347), scores -1.864, *below* null_p99, and FAILs; the f.86 gloss itself PASSes (-0.894). So
  at N about 150-170 and this transcription noise the judge does not recognise a decode known to come from the right key:
  **the judge is a non-test for this key at this N** (rule 3, the ZX-DEC349 and positive-control paragraphs). The c268 FAIL is
  "judge cannot decide", not a negative. What remains is control-backed: c268 decodes more French-like than all 100 of its own
  shuffles, and the published table reads it as French at least as well as it reads the glossed f.86.

  against the c268 decode gives 0.385 vs shuffle mean 0.357, p95 0.391: no match. c270 begins with the subscription "...De Varsovie
  ... d'avril 1573" closing the f.138 letter, then clear prose about Pybrac, Bellievre, the Admiral's cause and "la conspiraon", so it reads
  as the letter's clear continuation. Whether any later leaf carries the decipherment is untested.
- **Stop.** Per the brief, the run stops at the first failed control: the judge's positive control. Depth candidate: D1 at most
  ("fragments read" shape: "qui n'espargne(nt) nul ho[mme]" is one decode line, unconfirmed by any gloss).

tiles = B (t 5 of its 6 aligned; A t 1 of 5; C t 1 of 3); with the max group re-picked per shuffle, **p 0.063, null p95 5 = observed:
FAIL** (p >= 0.05). (Fixed-X=B p 0.049 is reported only; it is not the registered statistic.) Kappa against MONLUC-2's blind A/B
(B+C vs A collapsed): 10/14 agree, kappa 0.43. (3) *--try not run* (gate failed, per pre-registration); key.tsv, ciphertext_c172.tsv
unchanged.

`--try` finds 1 of 6 known letters on this 150-token decode, so on f.86 that condition is an instrument that cannot pass (rule 3: a
control that fails the same way as the target licenses nothing either way). Its reject of K69=t is therefore neither for nor against the
relabel, and this pass does **not** use `--try` for anything. The relabel rests on two things that are not `--try`: (a) **sign shape**, a
value-blind binary sort (no line, gloss, cell or value shown) that put 10 f.86 K07 tiles and the f.86 K38 tile in the curl-present
group, the same construction as the key sheet's K38 cell (C-curl round a z, table t) and as the c268 C-curl group (MONLUC-BLIND,
--try accept there with both nulls passed; MONLUC-RELABEL); and (b) **the leaf's own period gloss**, which faces those 10 tokens with
t 7 / n 1 / s 1 / none 1 and faces the not-curl K07 tiles with g 5 of 5 (pre-registered gate p 0.0086, MONLUC-CURL). This is a
departure from MONLUC-CURL's (3), stated here rather than hidden: that clause named an instrument now shown powerless, and the
relabel is a transcription correction by shape, not a value learned from --try.
- **What is relabelled, and why all 10.** The transcription follows the shape, not the gloss: all 10 blind curl-present tokens are
  written K38 (L02:1, L02:20, L02:31, L03:6, L03:19, L03:22, L03:30, L03:39, L03:45, L04:22). Relabelling only the 7 whose gloss reads
  t would choose a sign by its value. The U tile (L04:7, edge-cut) and the 6 not-curl tiles stay K07.
- **Grades (rule 4), from the gloss only.** C where the faced gloss letter is t (7: L02:1, L02:31, L03:6, L03:30, L03:39, L03:45,
  L04:22); M where it is not (L02:20 faces s, L03:19 faces n, L03:22 faces no gloss letter). No S or H is claimed for any of the 10.
- **Change.** `check_cells.py` carries a `RELABEL` table applied only when writing `ciphertext_c172.tsv`: the script asserts each old
  label is K07, writes K38, keeps the old label in `passA`/`passB` and marks `relabel` = `curl-sort` (new column). The alignment, the
  gloss letter each token faces, `cells_c172.tsv`, `results_cells_c172.json` and `votes_c172.tsv` are computed on the transcription
  before the relabel and are byte-identical to before (so the relabel cannot move which gloss letter a token faces, and MONLUC-KEY's
  per-cell numbers stand). key.tsv unchanged; c268 untouched; `conf` unchanged (both passes read the position; they had no K38/K07
  distinction to disagree on).
- **Decode, f.86 (150 tokens):** C 76 / M 71 / U 3 -> **C 83 / M 64 / U 3** (`decode_key.py --check`: "reading up to date", exit 0;
  `check_cells.py --check`: OK, all four outputs current). Ten letters change g -> t, e.g. L02 "gepmuig..." -> "tepmuig...",
  L03 "...uaugee g fau droi g qu..." -> "...uat tee t fau droi t qu...".
- **Judge rerun** (`python3 tools/judge_plaintext.py specs/fr4735-monluc-lansac-poland-1573.json --file <letters of reading_c172.txt>`,
  letters only, `[?vertical_bar]` dropped, N 147; scratch files):
  ```
  before: FAIL language: score=-1.864, null_p99=-1.752, real_p05=-0.91, real_median=-0.784, mode=both, N=147
          ok   words: cover=0.673, min=0.5, real_text_median_cover=0.946
  after:  FAIL language: score=-1.672, null_p99=-1.752, real_p05=-0.91, real_median=-0.784, mode=both, N=147
          ok   words: cover=0.701, min=0.5, real_text_median_cover=0.946
  gloss (gloss_c172_withline1.txt, normalised, N=145, same judge): PASS language: score=-0.894, null_p99=-1.738, real_p05=-0.926
  ```
  Still FAIL; the score moves from below the shuffled null's p99 to just above it, far short of real_p05. Reported, not a gate: the
  table decode still faces the gloss on only 83 of 150 tokens, and judge_n_c172.py's positive control FAILs at this N (Remaining gaps).

**What the leaves are.** ff.140r-141r are one clear letter in a clerk's hand, opening "Madame, nous sommes ..." (f.140r line 1), running over
f.140v (which ends a paragraph, then "Madame R..." and a short postscript-like block "Lon escrit ..." / "Depuis ceste lettre escripte ...
l'alarme du palatin de Sendomierz ...") and ending on f.141r line 11 ("... ce que je spere n'aviendra pas"), no signature, no date on the
leaves seen. f.142r is a different item (a Latin-style oration in French, "Si aucun de vous messieurs ...", on the election of a king). Contents
of f.140-141 mention l'empereur, le roy de Dannemarc, Monseigneur (Anjou), the palatins (Lasqui = Łaski, Sendomierz), "Balagny", the election:
an election-campaign report to the queen mother, consistent with item 54/55 (28 Apr 1573, to the queen mother) but **the date is not read on
the leaves**, so the identification with the 28 Apr letter is inferred from position (straight after the cipher pages ff.132-138) and content.

**Match against the cipher leaves' known clear words (c268 = f.138, lines 1-5, `reading_c268.txt`).** f.140r lines 6-11, read from the 1600 px
strip (my reading, M where marked ?):

| c268 decode (Tomokiyo key, K38 relabel) | f.140r clear (line) |
|---|---|
| L01 `les pe ruiteurs de qui np espargnent lhon` | l.7 "Les [pour?]suiteurs de lempereur qui ny espargnent honneur" |

| L05 `eeopilatirentnelaynex_lupionqqil` | l.9-10 "mort(?) ... Ilz tirent / de la une conclusion quil" |

Every one of the five c268 lines has its counterpart in sequence, including the c268 word gaps the decode could not read (L02 "conscience",

## MONL-ALIGN: ff.140r-141r transcribed and c268 lines 1-5 aligned against it (14 Oct 2026, account 4, Opus, for LANE DEFAULT-A4-0935-14)

Brief: .claude/briefs/runs/2026-10-14-acct4-default-0935-jobs.md, MONL-ALIGN (Wave 3). Read MONL-140 first (above). No print or
edition search was run: whether this clear letter is printed (Noailles, a Monluc or Lansac edition, Lettres de Catherine de Medicis)
is **not searched** in this job.

**Route and crops.** openapi.bnf.fr IIIF v3, `btv1b9060724s`, c272 = f.140r, c273 = f.140v, c274 = f.141r (MONL-140's map). 10 requests,
all HTTP 200 (3 info.json, 3 overviews at 1400 px, 4 native text regions, one c273 re-fetch to take in the last line). Line crops by
`tools/iiif_lines.py --iiif-host openapi --deskew-region --mask-neighbours` (the hand slopes about -2 to -3 deg; without deskew the
row profile found 27 of 30 lines on f.140r): f.140r 31 bands (30 lines + a blank), f.140v 26, f.141r 11, two segments each, 136
crops. All untracked (`images/scratchalign/`, self-ignoring .gitignore); manifest key `monlalign` in images/manifest.json;
`regen_monlalign.sh` re-derives every image and crop.

**Transcription (units: 6 blind Sonnet passes, one per page per pass, line crops only, + 1 reconciliation by this worker).**
passes/align/{f140r,f140v,f141r}_pass{A,B}.tsv. The passes are weak on this hand: word agreement A vs B (sequence match on folded
words) f.140r 265/409 = 0.648, f.140v 145/287 = 0.505, f.141r 88/135 = 0.652; both readers called their own pass low confidence
(18-123 bracketed words a page). Agreement, not accuracy. Reconciled clear text: `clear_f140.tsv` (68 lines; `how` column).
- f.140r L06-L10 (the lines facing c268): read by this worker's own eye from the line crops (`how` = eye). **Not blind**: I had read
  MONL-140's table and reading_c268.txt before reading these lines, so the three words I settled partly by context are bracketed
  ([poursuiteurs], [honneur], [encores]) and every token facing a bracketed letter gets no vote (below).
- Every other line: picked word by word from passes A and B by this worker without an image re-read (`how` = A/B); [?] where
  neither pass gave a plausible word. These lines are a rough clear text, good enough to locate the cipher span, not a reading.
- Content (from the reconciled text, unsure words aside): f.140r L01-L06 opens "Madame, nous sommes venuz du point ou se retrouuent
  les plaideurs quand le proces est sur le bureau ...", then L07-L10 "Les poursuiteurs de lempereur qui ny espargnent honneur, la face,
  la reputaon ny la conscience du roy. Lon faict parler le Roy de dannemarc qui ny pensa jamais, et donne a attendre quil est mort.
  Encores ilz tirent de la une conclusion quil est impossible que monseigneur sceust venir pardeca"; f.140v carries Balagny, "esleu Roy",
  the jewels, and after "Madame" a postscript "Depuis ceste lettre escripte jauoys eu lalarme du palatin de Sendomierz ..."; f.141r
  Lasqui (Laski), Sendomierz, ending "auec vne grande sedition (Ce que je spere naduiendra pas". No date or signature on the leaves.

**Alignment (`align_c268.py`, `--check` for rule 7).** I did not use `tools/interlinear_align.py`: its modes take numeral groups
(`align`) or a separate symbol stream with free per-symbol meanings (`stream`), whereas the question here is how a fixed published
table's letters sit against the clear text. align_c268.py is one dynamic-programming alignment of the whole c268 token stream
(ciphertext_c268.tsv, 177 tokens, pass A base with the K38 relabel) against a free local span of the whole 2,816-letter clear letter:
keyed token = one clear letter (+2 equal / -1 differs) or none (-2); a clear letter with no token -2; an unkeyed sign (?h_bar,
?double_slash) or W: word token takes 0-14 clear letters.
- **Where it lands:** f.140r L07-L10 (five c268 lines onto four clear lines). MONL-140's by-eye match is confirmed, and its l.6-11
  narrows to L07-L10. f.140r L01-L06 precede the c268 span, so they should face the end of c264 (f.136), which is not transcribed; not
  aligned here.
- **Statistic: share of keyed tokens whose table letter equals the clear letter they face.** Real 136/169 = **0.805** (contradict 28,
  extra 5). Controls: (1) shuffled clear (the letter's words in random order, same alignment, 200 draws, seed 140): mean 0.402, p95
  0.432, max 0.456, 0/200 at or above the real share; (2) a different clear letter, f.139 (c270_text.txt): 0.379. **Blindness check:**
  the same alignment against single blind pass A alone gives 0.615 and against pass B alone 0.621 (shuffled-clear max 0.468 / 0.438), both
  landing on f.140r L07-L10. So the location and the match do not depend on my non-blind reconciliation; the higher figure (0.805) does in
  part. The control can fail differently from the target: a wrong clear text moves the share, which (2) shows.
- **Tokens the clear text confirms under the current key (C):** 90 keyed tokens graded C through `decode_key.py --check`. These are tokens
  that read conf H, face their own table letter, and face a clear word read without brackets. Another 46 confirmations stay M: 33 are conf
  M tokens (an A/B split) and 13 face a bracketed clear word.
- **Tokens it contradicts:** 28 (21 conf H, 7 conf M), graded M. Three signs recur and are leads for an image check, not key edits:
  **K12** (table n) faces d at L02:36, L03:22, L04:15, L05:14 (each word-initial: "du", "de", "donne", "de la"), plus a/t in the
  L04 "pensa jamais ... a attendre" stretch where the alignment is loose; **K34** (table p) faces y at L01:22, L03:3, L03:21 ("ny", "roy",
  "ny"); **K14** (table p) faces s at L02:29, L04:5, L05:25 ("conscience", "pensa", "conclusion"). Each could be a pass A cell-id slip
  (look-alike cells) or a table reading error in the published key; the c268 image decides. The other contradictions are single, mostly
  in L04-L05 where the decode is weakest.
- **Name/code groups it fills (exceptions_c268.tsv):** W:mihil + W:Cur (L01:16-17) = "lempereur" (the clear "de lempereur qui"), graded
  C as a pair (mihil carries the word, Cur null). ?h_bar = c at all 3 occurrences (L02:8 "face", L02:34 "conscience", L03:32
  "dannemarc"), and ?double_slash = c twice in "conscience" (L02:26, L02:30). These 5 are graded M only because their tokens are conf M
  (uncertain_conf). The value is C-shaped; the sign identity is not settled. ?double_slash at L05:22 takes "onc" in "conclusion" (M:
  the chunk boundary is set by the alignment alone).
- **Grades (decode_key.py, job c268 in decode.json):** c268 lines 1-5, 177 tokens: **C 92 / M 85** (C = 90 confirmed keyed + the
  lempereur pair). `reading_c268_aligned.txt`, `reading_tokens_c268.tsv`; `python3 ../../tools/decode_key.py . --check` -> "reading up to
  date" (exit 0); `python3 align_c268.py --check` -> "align_c268: OK". score_c268.py's reading_c268.txt and its outputs are untouched.
- What this does not show: the remaining ~870 signs of c268, all of c264, and whether the clear text is a decipherment or the
  original draft. A judge run on the aligned reading was not done (the clear text, not the judge, is the evidence here).

y 586 of a 650 px source region, so its descenders are cut by the region itself and are not visible on disk.

**What the image shows (my eye, non-blind):**
| sign | positions | clear | shape seen | lead |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| K12 (n) | L02:36, L03:22, L04:15, L05:14 | d | a "q": bowl with a straight vertical stem. The confirmed-n K12 tokens beside them (L02 "m 9 F", "m 9 b", L03 "9 r", "9 o g") are a "9" whose tail sweeps left | pass A merged two cells: the straight-stem form is the table's K04 (d, drawn as a straight-stem 9); K04 is never labelled in c268 pass A. 3 of 4 clear on the image; L05:14 tail cut (also L05:12, an n, cannot be told apart there) |
| K14 (p) | L02:29, L04:5, L05:25 | s | a tall stem hooked over at the top with a closed bowl at the lower right ("b" with a hooked ascender). The confirmed-p K14 at L02:14 is a different, "ß"-like form | pass A merged two forms under K14; the hooked one is nearest the table's K17 (s), which pass A never labels in c268. The same hooked form is L01:4, which MONL-ALIGN aligned to the bracketed "[poursuiteurs]": read as s, L01:1-12 decode "les s e r v i t e u r s" = **"les serviteurs" de lempereur**, so the bracketed clear word is probably wrong at f.140r L07 |
| K34 (p) | L01:22, L03:3, L03:21 | y ("ny", "roy", "ny") | a small "ß" whose foot sweeps down-left; the confirmed-p K34 (L01:25 "esp-") is upright with a straight stem | weak: the sweeping form is closer to K53 (i, a long-tailed beta), which would read "ni", "roi", "ni"; the two beta cells are close on the table and in the hand |

**--try (its own controls; key.tsv never written; logged in crossword_log.tsv, flag non-blind).**
`python3 tools/decode_key.py ciphers/fr4735-monluc-lansac-poland-1573 --try K12=d,K14=s,K34=y --try-log ciphers/fr4735-monluc-lansac-poland-1573/crossword_log.tsv`

## MONL-R0S: positional null for the code-wide K14 = s --try (15 Oct 2026, account 1, Opus, for LANE DEFAULT-A1-1426-15)

Brief: .claude/briefs/runs/2026-10-15-acct1-default-1426-jobs.md, MONL-R0S (MONL-SORT2's Verdict). Disk only: no host requests, no subagents, no looks.
**Pre-registration:** k14try/r0s/PREREG-MONL-R0S.md pushed in 3f022d097 before any scoring. Why the tool's null failed: tools/decode_key.py --try null 1 needs 51 positions currently read p outside K14; only 24 exist (K34, K47).
**Null (k14try/r0s/run_r0s.py, imports decode_key's Crossword, fr16, window 8):** 51 positions drawn without replacement from every one-letter, non-s, non-K14 position (N1, pool 886; N2 = consonants only, pool 404), read as a pseudo-code valued p, statistic s minus p; 200 draws, Random(1573). It changes each occurrence's neighbours, so it can differ from the target (rule 3); it measures the s-over-p preference French gives at a random non-s spot.
**Implementation note:** the first run compared against lowercase 'p'/'s', but Crossword folds values to upper case (u -> V), so the s exclusion and the vowel filter did nothing and the background p was not a model letter; that run (which printed LICENSED) is discarded as a bug against the PREREG, fixed (lm_fold), re-run once. No pool, seed or draw count was changed.
`python3 -I k14try/r0s/run_r0s.py` -> k14try/r0s/results.tsv:
| run | codes | n | statistic (bits s over p) | N1 mean / p95 / p | N2 mean / p95 / p | lose > 3 bits |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R0s** | K14 | 51 | **+554.0** | 404.5 / **578.3** / 0.065 | 444.7 / 595.8 / 0.115 | 5 |
| PC (power) | K17+K37+K67 (table s) set to p | 29 | +414.8 | 199.3 / 294.2 / 0.000 | 222.0 / 321.7 / 0.000 | 0 |
| NC (negative) | K34+K47 (table p) | 24 | +118.5 | 163.6 / 264.5 / 0.810 | 180.2 / 264.4 / 0.875 | 4 |
Value-class null (decode_key try_value, 200 draws): p95 518.1 (R0s above it). R0s's statistic is +554.0 here, +549.8 in MONL-K14TRY (MONL-SORT2's relabels changed neighbours since).
**Registered decision: REFUSED.** PC above both null p95s (power at n 29), NC below the N1 p95 (control passes), R0s at or below the N1 p95 (554.0 <= 578.3): K14's code-wide preference for s is no greater than a random non-s code's, from letter frequency alone. K14 stays p in key.tsv (unchanged); no lead file written. This does not undo MONL-K14TRY's T14 (the 19 s-facing K14, selected by the clear text, read s by context) or MONL-SORT2's shape (K14, not a K37 merge): it says the code as a whole is not s, which leaves a split value or a table-placement question at the s-facing occurrences.
Checks: `python3 -I k14try/r0s/run_r0s.py --check` "run_r0s: OK"; `python3 ../../tools/decode_key.py . --check` "reading up to date". Box 14:40-14:5x UTC 15 Oct by date -u. Requests: none. Subagents: none. Own looks: none. Cost not visible to this session.
